# 第 8 章　幻造：圣杯战争

*4.4 版本主视觉　·　图源：米游社*

> Imagenae: Holy Grail War (Trailblaze Continuance)　·　幻造：圣杯战争

## 051　The Opening Battle　开幕之初战

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Dovebrook District　|　角色：Trailblazer; Furbulitzer; Poofy; Snowie; Rin Tohsaka; Gilgamesh; Lost Emerald Chimera; Lancer; Kuchiba; Castorice; Sparxie; "Narrator"; Ashveil; Svarog　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Arcadia Revisited'' completed

### Story Recap

**(Trailblazer):** Under the guise of a variety show, Old Oti orchestrated an unprecedented "Holy Grail War" in Penacony. Seven Masters were to summon illustrious figures from history as Servants, all vying for the chance to grant any wish.

> **开拓者：**老奥帝以综艺节目为幌子，举办了一场空前盛大的匹诺康尼「圣杯战争」。七名御主将召唤历史上的杰出名人作为从者，争夺实现一切愿望的机会。

**(Trailblazer):** Having survived the relentless assaults by Skott and Lancer, a twist of fate allowed me to summon a Servant from another world: Saber, Altria. This otherworldly hero, known as "King Arthur," pledged herself to our alliance.

> **开拓者：**挺过斯科特与Lancer的连番袭击，我于机缘巧合中召唤出了不属于这个世界的从者——Saber阿尔托莉雅，被称为亚瑟王的异界英雄，并与众人结成同盟。

**(Trailblazer):** We set out to pursue Old Oti's lackey, Skott, and uncover the conspiracy simmering beneath the War, only to find ourselves ensnared in Assassin Grady's Noble Phantasm.

> **开拓者：**我们决定追击受老奥帝雇佣的斯科特，解除潜藏在圣杯战争水面之下的阴谋，却反被关进了Assassin葛瑞迪的宝具。

**(Trailblazer):** Within his Bounded Field, the quick wits of Aventurine and Archer allowed us to expose Grady's weakness and strike him down. But the victory was bitter: Robin's Servant, Caster Miss Note had fallen under his influence and turned her back on us.

> **开拓者：**在结界中，多亏砂金和Archer的聪明才智，我们才能找出葛瑞迪的弱点，将其击溃。可惜知更鸟的从者Caster音符小姐却受他蛊惑，离开了我们。

**(Trailblazer):** As we reached the Grand Theater, Miss Note stood in our path and plunged us into an illusion of an endless, mediocre life. Side by side, Saber and I shattered the illusion, finally standing face-to-face with the man behind it all: Old Oti.

> **开拓者：**我们赶到大剧院，遭到了音符小姐阻拦，在幻觉中陷入了一场无休无止的平庸人生。最终我和Saber携手打破幻境，来到了罪魁祸首老奥帝的面前。

**(Trailblazer):** To our surprise, his attempt to summon the "Self" within our hearts manifested as a monstrous parasite. With our combined efforts, both Berserker and the Holy Grail were reduced to ash.

> **开拓者：**出人意料的是，他试图召唤出人们心中的「自己」，却以蛀虫的姿态降临。众人齐心协力，将Berserker和圣杯双双化作灰烬。

**(Trailblazer):** With the crisis averted, the Servants bid their final goodbyes, their forms slowly dissolving from this world. The shattered Holy Grail, though restored, has lost its resonance with the memoria, remaining with me only as a silent relic... But little did I know, it would soon spark a new storm...

> **开拓者：**危机解除，从者们纷纷消散，与新朋旧友告别。被击碎的圣杯修复后失去了响应忆质的功能，成为了我的所有物…但，谁能料到，它将惹来新的风波——

### Ask Furbulitzer about the situation

> *Before officially introducing the Imagenae Holy Grail War, I must first introduce you all to the protagonist of this Holy Grail War, I mean the protagonist of this galaxy aside from myself, (Trailblazer)... ...Ugh, what a dreadful opening. No one sees this until it's polished to perfection.*

[Recap] You returned to Furbobo Press, where Furbulitzer asked you to watch the latest episode of the Arcadian Morning Report. There's important news.

*(Talk to Furbulitzer)*

**Furbulitzer:** How terrible... Ah, you're back! If I may ask, are you planning to write an article, draft an article, or revise an article?

> **普狸策：**糟糕哩…啊，您回来哩！请问您是打算写稿、撰稿、还是改稿哩？

> **普狸策：**…糟糕哩。

**(Trailblazer):** No, I mean, what is terrible?

> **开拓者：**我问的是什么事情糟糕哩。

- ▸ That's all writing. Can't I just slack off?

**Furbulitzer:** ...Ah, of course you can slack off and think about happy things.

> **普狸策：**…啊，您当然可以多偷偷懒，多想想开心的事哩。

**(Trailblazer):** Are you implying that there are unhappy things to think about, too?

> **开拓者：**难道还有让我不开心的事？

- ▸ ...I already know what happened.

**Furbulitzer:** You already know!? If I'd known that, I wouldn't have tried to hide it.

> **普狸策：**您已经知道真相哩？！早知道就不隐瞒哩。

**(Trailblazer):** Well, I hadn't always known...

> **开拓者：**本来我是不知道的……

**Furbulitzer:** No point hiding it... Then this little furbo will stop trying to protect your mental well-being, Boss. Time to go all out and hold nothing back...

> **普狸策：**藏不住了哩…那小狸我也不顾忌社长大人的心理防线，发狠哩忘情哩豁出去哩——

**Furbulitzer:** Please watch the latest edition of Arcadian Morning Report!

> **普狸策：**请看最新一期《乐园早知道》哩——！

[Recap] According to reports, valuable goods were raining down across Planarcadia, and citizens were advised to watch out for falling objects from above. A major theft of exhibited items occurred at the Star Rail FEST; the Security Bureau had high praise for the thieves' professional skills.

**Poofy:** We interrupt our regular programming for breaking news!

> **噗噗：**现在紧急插播一条新闻——

**Snowie:** Planarcadia's weather station has issued a Code Gold Rainstorm Warning: Over the past six hours, valuable goods, including precious metals, rare artwork, and jewelry, have continuously fallen across the entire arcadia.

> **雪雪：**二相乐园气象台发布金色暴雨预警：过去六小时，乐园全境持续降下包括贵金属、珍贵艺术品及珠宝首饰在内的贵重货物。

**Poofy:** Citizens are advised to beware of falling objects. Please stay indoors and refrain from scavenging, to avoid looting-related incidents.

> **噗噗：**请市民谨防高空坠物，切勿外出捡拾，避免哄抢引发的次生灾害。

**Poofy:** Didn't know that this counted as weather, or that a warning could be issued after the rain's stopped. Let's move on to the next story...

> **噗噗：**第一次知道这算气象，预警还能在雨下完发。让我们回到下一条新闻——

**Snowie:** Today, a major theft of exhibited items occurred at the Star Rail FEST currently being held on the skyline's Lookout Cloud.

> **雪雪：**今日，在天际线观览云岛持续展出的「星铁FES」发生重大展品盗窃案。

**Snowie:** The Security Bureau has high praises for the thieves' professional skills: According to our source, the theft was "non-violent and incredibly disciplined."

> **雪雪：**治安局对窃贼业务水平赞赏有加：相关人士称，这起盗窃「没有暴力，非常专业」。

**Poofy:** We'll be right back after a commercial break...

> **噗噗：**接下来插播一段广告……

**Furbulitzer:** That's just unprofessional. They skipped right over the most important part!

**Furbulitzer:** How impressive! Realizing you're the victim right away!

**Furbulitzer:** Our field reporters are rushing to the scene right now, but...

> **普狸策：**外勤记者正在紧急出动，不过……

(I): The Fifth Holy Grail War

... Fifth Holy Grail War Holy Grail Descent Location Ryuudou Temple, Fuyuki City Combatants Team Saber Master: Shirou Emiya An ordinary high schooler with a straightforward personality. A good-natured guy with a strong sense of justice. A Saber-class Servant. A knight who champions the weak and stands against the mighty, earnest and resolute in all things. Team Archer Master: Rin Tohsaka One of the administrators of Fuyuki City, and the current head of the Tohsaka family. That would be me. An Archer-class Servant. A Servant whose true identity remains a mystery. Cool-headed and pragmatic by nature. Sports gray hair. Though classified as an Archer, he is also an expert in a wide array of other weapons. Team Berserker Master: Illyasviel von Einzbern A petite girl with beautiful long silver-white hair. Don't let the looks fool you. She's got a surprisingly sharp tongue and a bit of a little devil in her. A Berserker-class Servant. A legendary hero of myth. Blessed with immense life force and an iron will, he stands as one of the most powerful Servants in this Holy Grail War. Team Caster Master: Soichiro Kuzuki A teacher of few words with an air of quiet austerity. A Caster-class Servant, she's the wickedly brilliant grand witch of legend, passionate in both love and hate. She is clad in a purple robe that fans out like a bat's wings. Team Assassin Servant: Kojirou Sasaki A swordmaster Assassin-class Servant. His Master is not one of the seven Masters of this Holy Grail War. It can be said that his very existence is a highly anomalous case among the many Holy Grail War rituals. Team Rider Master: Shinji Matou The eldest son of the Matou family, one of the three great mage families based in Fuyuki. Has plenty of talent, except when it comes to magecraft. A Rider-class Servant. Once worshiped as a goddess in myth, now depicted as a monster incapable of making contact with others. Other Factions Team Lancer & Overseer Master: Kirei Kotomine A member of the clergy with a saintly aura. From his position as an Overseer, he should theoretically avoid intervening in the conflict, but... Two Servants are present simultaneously: Lancer and Archer. Why there are two Archers, you ask? Well, that is another story entirely. But if it concerns the oldest King of Heroes, the one who holds all the treasures and wisdom in the world, nothing he does should come as a surprise. ...

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Lookout Cloud Station　|　角色：Trailblazer

### Investigate the crime scene

> *Before officially introducing the Imagenae Holy Grail War, I must first introduce you all to the protagonist of this Holy Grail War, I mean the protagonist of this galaxy aside from myself, (Trailblazer)... ...Ugh, what a dreadful opening. No one sees this until it's polished to perfection.*

[Recap] The place was a total wreck. You couldn't even remember what used to be on display here, so you decided to look around first.

**(Trailblazer):** (What a mess... I can't recall what was on display.)

**(Trailblazer):** (...I should look around.)

*(Investigate fallen display case in the back, optional)*

- ▸ Investigate

...Some kind of gemstone dust. If a thief did this, they applied a bit too much pressure.

*(Investigate standing display case to the right)*

- ▸ Investigate

[Recap] The label on the empty display case read: Simulated "Holy Grail." Seemed like this was the stolen item. If you want something done right, do it yourself, so you immediately started an investigation to track down the thief's whereabouts.

The display case is completely empty.

The display label reads: "Pseudo 'Holy Grail.' A treasure claimed by (Trailblazer) during the Penacony Holy Grail War. It has since lost its ability to grant wishes and summon Heroic Spirits within a memoria environment."

- ▸ Is something as valuable as the Holy Grail even insured?

If you don't remember it, then it didn't happen.

What I mean by "no guarantees" is: If it can't be recovered, nobody's paying you back.

- ▸ Who in their right mind would go out of their way to steal a Holy Grail?

You have a long list in your mind of "who exactly the thief is"...

But after considering some grown-up realities, you quickly narrowed it down: no one.

- ▸ Could this be... a clever little detail to trigger a collaboration?

How ingeniously clever!

Sadly, this universe plays by strict rules — certain exceptions aside — and a little creative thinking is hardly enough to kick off a Holy Grail War.

The security officers are overwhelmed and run off their feet, but their investigative prowess doesn't hold a candle to yours.

If you want a job done right, you have to do it yourself... Better start the investigation immediately and track down the thief's whereabouts.

*(Investigate the gemstone)*

- ▸ Investigate

[Recap] A gem scattered on the ground seemed to whisper: "I am inextricably linked to the thief of the Holy Grail." You picked it up and felt an indescribable strength flowing within the gem.

Amid the bustling crowds of Star Rail FEST, a perfectly lustrous gemstone has fallen, catching only your eye.

Did no one else notice? Or are they simply mistaking it for an exhibit?

It seems to possess a certain magic, capturing your gaze and gnawing at your curiosity.

It said: "I am inextricably linked to the thief of the Holy Grail."

- ▸ Pick it up for a closer look.

Maybe we'll turn up some clues.

- ▸ It's a trap!

Your internal alarms are blaring, yet your hands, seasoned from years of scavenging, act of their own accord

- ▸ Thank you, bounty of nature!

"Yoink!"

The moment you touch it, you realize it is no inanimate object, but that some indescribable power flows within the gem...

Without a doubt, this trap was set up specifically for you.

*(Quick Read)*

(II): Root and Origin

... Root and Origin What is Root? Like a book, it records the causes, processes, and ends of the infinite things in the universe. An empty cosmos, and magecraft included, owes its very existence to this item, hence the name "Root." It's not hard to see that it is both "infinite knowledge" and "the start of an empty cosmos," a walking contradiction. And so, the so-called "Root" is really just a concept humans invented to wrap their heads around the idea of infinity. After all, if infinity is going to mean anything, you need a boundary to define it. Otherwise, the very distinction between finite and infinite loses all meaning. What is Origin? If Root is the primordial "cause" of the cosmos itself, then Origin is the individual "cause" behind every single thing that exists within the cosmos. It's like an arrow, fixing the direction of development the moment something comes into being. No living being can defy this directionality. As the saying goes, "You reap what you sow." Yet living beings are rarely aware of where their Origin lies. Once awakened, one's consciousness becomes dominated by the Origin, until it becomes the Origin itself. ...

**Rin Tohsaka:** I'm a bit surprised myself!

> **远坂凛：**我也很意外啦…！

**Rin Tohsaka:** In any case, let's take stock of the situation.

> **远坂凛：**总之，先盘点下现状吧。

**Rin Tohsaka:** First, the detective deduced that the true culprit wasn't Sparxie, but Ishtar hiding behind her. That's why he feigned betrayal: to try and win Sparxie over.

> **远坂凛：**第一，侦探先生推理出了真凶不是火花，而是她背后的伊什塔尔，这才假意叛变，试图策反她。

**Rin Tohsaka:** Second, in order to win the war for herself, Sparxie wanted to incite the detective to fight the goddess, and thus agreed to meet with him.

> **远坂凛：**第二，为了胜利，火花想要借刀背刺女神，这才同意和他见面。

**Rin Tohsaka:** Third, each harboring their own motives, the two decided to work together, using their meeting as a trap to lure the goddess out.

> **远坂凛：**第三，貌合神离的两人一拍即合，假借会面设下圈套，引诱女神出面……

**Rin Tohsaka:** Too bad both of those idiots mistook me for the goddess. They never thought that there could be another me in the world — though, to be fair, neither did I.

> **远坂凛：**可惜，这两个大笨蛋都把我当成了女神，从来没想过世界上还有另一个我——虽然我也没想到就是了。

**Ashveil:** Haha, easy now, easy.

> **不死途：**哈哈，息怒，息怒。

**Rin Tohsaka:** You... I'm surprised Archer didn't catch you.

> **远坂凛：**你这家伙…居然没被Archer逮住呢。

**Ashveil:** Home turf advantage. I have plenty of tricks up my sleeve.

> **不死途：**主场作战，我有的是办法。

**Ashveil:** Here, the elixir of youth. Please, take it and forgive me.

> **不死途：**给，返老还童药——还请各位高抬贵手。

**Ashveil:** This elixir is the real deal. But I have a feeling using it to extend my life would bring some serious bad karma, so I haven't dared to touch a single drop.

> **不死途：**这药货真价实。不过，总感觉用它延寿会遭报应，我是一滴都没敢动。

**Lancer:** You're surprisingly principled about this sort of thing. What about your Master? He doesn't strike me as the type to resist temptation.

> **Lancer：**这种事上倒是挺有原则。那你御主呢？他可不像能制服诱惑的人。

**Ashveil:** Mr. Won? Anyone with eyes can see he doesn't have a single good intention. It would only be a matter of time before he showed his true colors.

> **不死途：**老万嘛…明眼人都能看出来他一肚子坏水，流出来只是时间早晚问题。

**Ashveil:** So I took the initiative to dissolve our contract. Without his imagenated body, he probably reverted back to a halo.

> **不死途：**所以，我主动解除了契约。失去幻造身体，他大概变回天环了吧。

**Ashveil:** That guy was definitely interested in the elixir. He said, "I finally thought of the right way to fly with my ear wings. I want to go back to when I was a kid and try it out"...

> **不死途：**那家伙确实对灵药非常感兴趣，说「终于想到了用耳羽飞行的正确方法，想回到小时候试一试」……

**Ashveil:** Well, he won't get the chance now. You're welcome.

> **不死途：**现在他没机会了，感谢我吧。

**Rin Tohsaka:** Uhh...

> **远坂凛：**呃……

**Ashveil:** Look on the bright side: at least we lured out the true culprit and figured out what she's up to.

> **不死途：**往好处想：我们至少引出了真凶，还搞明白了她在打什么算盘。

**Ishtar:** It's too late for that realization. My rituals have already spread across Planarcadia. If I so wish, I can claim the Wishpower permeating this land at any moment.

> **伊什塔尔：**现在意识到已经晚了。我的仪式已经遍布二相乐园，只要我愿意，随时都能将弥漫在这片土地的愿力收入囊中——

**Ishtar:** Hand over the Holy Grail and put an end to this farce!

> **伊什塔尔：**交出圣杯，结束这场闹剧吧！

**Rin Tohsaka:** That shameless goddess kept bragging how she could end the War in an instant, only to run away with her tail between her legs... I guess she was bluffing, just like me.

> **远坂凛：**那个大言不惭的家伙，口口声声说自己能瞬间结束圣杯战争，结果夹着尾巴跑了…看来是和我一样，也在虚张声势呢。

**Lancer:** But she's a goddess nonetheless. Even if she hasn't recovered her full strength and might be at a disadvantage when outnumbered, taking out a mere Servant would be child's play for her, let alone a Master.

> **Lancer：**但神明毕竟是神明。就算还没获得完整力量，面对一对多的局面会落得下风，但收拾区区一个从者还是不在话下，更不必说御主了。

**Lancer:** Speaking of which, she seemed remarkably interested in that Holy Grail of yours.

> **Lancer：**说起这个——她似乎对你手里的圣杯关心有加啊。

**Rin Tohsaka:** The Holy Grail is no longer a wish-granting chalice... So what does she want with it?

> **远坂凛：**圣杯已经不再是许愿机了…她要这个做什么？

**Lancer:** Is it possible that, like you, what she wants isn't the Grail itself, but the magical energy inside?

> **Lancer：**有没有一种可能：和你一样，她要的不是圣杯，而是里面的魔力？

**Rin Tohsaka:** You mean... she wants to break free from the constraints of Wishpower and forge a true divine body for herself?

> **远坂凛：**你是说…她想挣脱愿力束缚，为自己打造真正的神躯？

**Kuchiba:** Spot on.

> **朽叶：**猜对了。

**Kuchiba:** She left a challenge: We are to hand over the Holy Grail at the Star Rail FEST before the seventh day.

> **朽叶：**她留了挑战书，勒令我们在第七天到来前，把圣杯乖乖交到星铁FES……

**Kuchiba:** Otherwise, she'll start the ritual and take the Holy Grail herself.

> **朽叶：**否则，她会启动仪式，亲自夺走圣杯。

- ▸ Now we're in real trouble.
- ▸ It's not my first time slaying a god.

**Rin Tohsaka:** At this point... we have no choice but to face her head-on.

> **远坂凛：**到了这份上…只能正面应战了啊。

### Check on Rin

[Recap] Being enemies with Archer left Rin Tohsaka feeling a little down. With your encouragement, Rin Tohsaka pulled herself together, turning memories of the past into the strength to move forward.

*(Talk to Rin Tohsaka)*

**Rin Tohsaka:** I never thought I'd get dragged into the eye of the storm like this, with Archer on the opposing side.

> **远坂凛：**真没想到会这样卷入旋涡中心，Archer还站在对面……

**Rin Tohsaka:** Of all the people to end up in this kind of drama... Fate has a twisted sense of irony.

> **远坂凛：**…不一样。或者说，有本质区别。

> **远坂凛：**当局者迷旁观者清，但只有亲身体会过两种立场的人，才能明白其中真正复杂的问题。

> **远坂凛：**原来开拓者偶尔也会说这种深刻的话。

**Rin Tohsaka:** I will. It's a story about Archer and me... a story of reality and ideals.

> **远坂凛：**会的。有关我和Archer…一个现实和理想的故事。

**Rin Tohsaka:** ...

> **远坂凛：**……

**Rin Tohsaka:** Everyone, pull yourselves together. We're going to be very busy from here on out.

> **远坂凛：**各位，打起精神吧。接下来我们恐怕有得忙了。

**Rin Tohsaka:** I swear on the honor of the Tohsaka family: I will do everything I can to bring this event to a satisfactory end.

> **远坂凛：**以远坂家的荣誉起誓：我会全力以赴，让事件以最圆满的方式落下帷幕。

**Rin Tohsaka:** I will defeat her. Both the goddess, and this "perfect" version of myself.

> **远坂凛：**我会战胜她。战胜女神。战胜更完美的自己。

**Lancer:** There's the Tohsaka I know.

> **Lancer：**我熟悉的远坂又回来了。

**Rin Tohsaka:** My head feels much clearer. I can feel my connection to this land growing stronger.

> **远坂凛：**…头脑清晰了许多。我能感觉到，我和土地的连结更紧密了。

**Rin Tohsaka:** As I am now, perhaps I can turn the memories of the past into the strength to move forward.

> **远坂凛：**现在的我…或许能将过去的记忆，化作前进的力量。

- ▸ I wanna be a knight of the Goddess too.

**Argenti:** But after that elegant, magnificent, and daring contest, we cleared up our misunderstanding.

**Argenti:** She is only fulfilling the wishes of a girl named Rin Tohsaka, and helping her find her most precious object.

**Rin Tohsaka:** ...

## 055　Assault! Seafeld TV　突击！海原电视台

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Seafeld TV Tower　|　角色：Trailblazer; Rin Tohsaka; Castorice; Sparxie; Ashveil; Wonweek; Ishtar; Archer

### Track down the traitor and the mastermind

> *The betrayal committed by Mr. Detective was unexpected, yet it made perfect sense. After all, a Holy Grail War would not be complete without mutual scheming and backstabbing. However, what no one could have anticipated was that the scheme within a scheme of Mr. Detective would actually reel in a completely unexpected big fish...*

[Recap] The staff had all collapsed to the ground, and Rin Tohsaka sensed "Death" in the air. To mitigate the effects of the Bounded Field, Rin Tohsaka decided to use a magecraft that switched the background music.

**Rin Tohsaka:** Their vitals are stable, it's like they're asleep. Is this the effect of "tuning"?

**Rin Tohsaka:** ...!

**Rin Tohsaka:** It's the scent of "Death."

**Sweet Murmurs:** Please, look back for me...

**Rin Tohsaka:** Of course, it's merely a Bounded Field.

**Rin Tohsaka:** Is "looking back" the rule of this Bounded Field?

**Rin Tohsaka:** In essence, a Bounded Field imposes rules advantageous to the caster within a specific area. We figure the rules out, we can minimize its effects.

**Rin Tohsaka:** This Bounded Field echoes with calls that seem to interfere with mental stability. If we obey the voice and turn around, the consequences would likely be disastrous.

**Rin Tohsaka:** Which also means that as long as the voice doesn't reach our ears, this Bounded Field will fall apart on its own.

(Changes BGM to Solo Maxxing Instrumental)

### Help the reluctant Miss Rider escape from the den

[Recap] You and Rin Tohsaka entered the station director's office, which had transformed into a sea of flowers. Ashveil asked Sparxie to fight you two, but Sparxie stepped back and coerced Castorice into fighting you two instead.

**Rin Tohsaka:** Believing I won't lose anything in the end is how I made it this far. And I will walk with the same belief on this path ahead—

> **远坂凛：**正是因为坚信什么都不会失去，我才一路走到了这里。接下来的路，我也会一如既往——

> **远坂凛：**最重要的是，我们从对彼此一无所知，变成了能向对方敞开心扉的存在。如果没有我这位可靠的从者，只靠我一个人，是绝对无法一步步走到这里的——

**(Trailblazer):** This unyielding "bond" is our greatest weapon.

## 057　Drink Up! King's Banquet　命运之长夜

> 世界：Planarcadia　|　区域：Phantasmoon Courtyard　|　角色：Trailblazer; Rin Tohsaka; Phainon; Saber; Gilgamesh; Lost Emerald Chimera; Lancer; Kuchiba; Archer

### Join everyone at the king's banquet

> *And thus, all seven Master-Servant pairs, a total of fourteen heroes, have made their appearance. The heroes destined to stand against the goddess are now fully assembled. As for Uruk's Great Goblet, the king will personally use it to host a banquet, offering a grand send-off for the final battle.*

[Recap] You and Rin Tohsaka pushed the door open and found yourselves inside the Phantasmoon Courtyard, a space conjured by Gilgamesh's Bounded Field. Gilgamesh beckoned everyone forward, inviting you all to join him in the grand endeavor of slaying a goddess.

**Phainon:** So, this is what the office looks like?

> **白厄：**事务所里居然长这样…？

**Gilgamesh:** This is just a Bounded Field, the tip of the iceberg when it comes to the Holy Grail.

> **吉尔伽美什：**只是结界，圣杯原典的冰山一角罢了。

**Gilgamesh:** This appears to be the "Phantasmoon Courtyard," a forbidden area of the Phantasmoon Games. It may not be as luxurious as my royal palace, but it is fairly elegant. This tree in particular suits me very well.

> **吉尔伽美什：**这似乎是幻月游戏的禁地「幻月秘庭」。虽然不及本王王宫奢华，倒也风雅——尤其是这棵大树，和我尤其相称。

**Gilgamesh:** I have no reason to grant you common folk the honor of witnessing a king's true might... but I can make an exception, to defeat that incompetent goddess.

> **吉尔伽美什：**虽说本王绝无理由向你们这群俗人开恩，见证王之威能…但既然要对付那位无能女神，破格一次也未尝不可。

**Gilgamesh:** Come forth — join me in the glorious slaying of a god!

> **吉尔伽美什：**上前来吧——与本王共襄弑神之盛举！

*(Approach Gilgamesh)*

[Recap] At the banquet, Kuchiba detected a taste of "fear of loss" in Gilgamesh's secret flavorings. Phainon invited Gilgamesh to recount his past, even if the price of hearing was to bear the weight of sin.

**Phainon:** A feast before slaying a god... Heh, that Imperator immediately comes to mind.

> **白厄：**弑神前的壮行宴…呵，难免想到那位凯撒。

**Gilgamesh:** Enough with the unpleasant talk. You should be drinking your fill.

> **吉尔伽美什：**别说不解风情的话了，现在是开怀畅饮的时候。

**Gilgamesh:** Tell me, how does my stash compared to the treasures of this world? Speak your mind freely.

> **吉尔伽美什：**都说说吧：本王的秘藏，相较这个世界如何？直抒己见便是。

**Gilgamesh:** Since some of you have yet to come of age... Fret not, there are non-alcoholic drinks.

> **吉尔伽美什：**考虑到有人没成年…放心，完全不含酒精。

**Kuchiba:** ...I'll grab a drink then.

> **朽叶：**…那我就不客气了。

**Kuchiba:** It perfectly balances the richness of honey, the sweetness of lilac and vanilla, and the tartness of citrus. I can even feel the dense and vast warmth of soil, like I'm stepping into a spring garden hanging in midair...

> **朽叶：**完美驾驭了蜂蜜的浓醇、丁香和香草荚的甜润和柑橘的酸爽，还有泥土般绵长厚重的暖意，仿佛踏入了一座建立在空中的春日花园……

**Kuchiba:** An exquisite flavor. Though, I can sense a "fear of loss."

> **朽叶：**风味确实极品——只可惜，有股「对失去的恐惧」。

**Gilgamesh:** Hahahaha! Certainly, it is the "fear of loss." Without the right amount of bitterness, treasures lose their value...

> **吉尔伽美什：**哈哈哈哈哈！不错，「对失去的恐惧」。没有恰到好处的苦涩，珍藏就无以为珍藏……

**Gilgamesh:** "Elation" — the foundation of all things — is another name for it.

> **吉尔伽美什：**「欢愉」——这便是万物的根本——它的另一个名字。

**Phainon:** Legendary King of Heroes, do you fear "loss" too?

> **白厄：**传说中的英雄王，也会对「失去」感到恐惧吗？

**Gilgamesh:** This concerns my heavy past. If I speak of it, you shall have to bear this sin for the rest of your life.

> **吉尔伽美什：**事关本王惹人忌讳的过去。只要开口，你就会背起永生无以偿还的罪孽。

**Phainon:** It'll just be one of many things on my shoulders. I'm all ears.

> **白厄：**我背负的还少吗？洗耳恭听。

[Recap] Born of both human and divine blood, Gilgamesh came into being from the gods' fear of humanity, yet would later join his dearest friend to overthrow those very gods. It was only after his friend's death that Gilgamesh could truly confront his origin as the "Wedge of the Heavens." As for the treasures, Kuchiba felt the King's Vintage held no real meaning for her. Phainon wished to reunite with his companions in the present world, but had no desire to make a wish with the King's Goblet for a fleeting dream that would be gone in the blink of an eye for everyone.

**Gilgamesh:** Hmph, reckless fools who have no concept of sin. But I shan't spoil the merrymaking, so listen closely.

> **吉尔伽美什：**哼，真是不知罪孽为何物的狂人啊。为免扫了酒兴，你们就竖起耳朵，洗耳恭听吧。

**Gilgamesh:** The union of "loss" and "gain" is the law. In the past, the gods were enforcers of the law. They controlled the balance of "loss" and "gain," thus preserving their rule.

> **吉尔伽美什：**「失」与「得」的统合即是法。过去，诸神就是法的代行者。他们掌管「失」与「得」的配平，由此延续统治。

**Gilgamesh:** However, the gods created humans. Unlike their creators, human desires knew no bounds. The world morphed to suit their desires. With enough time, they could even alter the rules of planets...

> **吉尔伽美什：**直到神发明了人类。与神不同，人类的欲望无边无际。世界的面貌会按照他们的欲求变化。只要时间足够，他们甚至能篡改星球本身的规则……

**Gilgamesh:** The gods foresaw that the law they defended would one day be overturned. Hence, they came to experience the "fear of loss."

> **吉尔伽美什：**众神预见到它们捍卫的法终将被推翻。于是，神感到了「对失去的恐惧」。

**Phainon:** Interesting. Despite being gods, most of the Titans of Amphoreus chose to walk alongside humans.

> **白厄：**有意思。同样是神，翁法罗斯的泰坦大多选择了与人类同行。

**Kuchiba:** Even the Aeons are hardly hostile toward humans... However, THEY simply regard humans as ants too insignificant to be considered enemies.

> **朽叶：**就算是星神也几乎不会对人类产生敌意…不过，在祂们看来，人类只是蝼蚁罢了，没有为敌的价值。

**Gilgamesh:** The gods could not match humans in numbers and desires. Thus, they were forced to merge both and create shackles that would bind humans while embodying both perspectives.

> **吉尔伽美什：**面对数量和欲望都难以抗衡的人类，它们只得破格与其结合，妄图创造同时拥有双方视角的、束缚人类的枷锁。

**Gilgamesh:** As long as they forsook their dignity, they could continue to rule over the law. This was the optimal "loss" and "gain" they could come up with.

> **吉尔伽美什：**只要舍弃尊严，它们便能继续主宰万法——这就是它们能想到的，最划算的「失」与「得」。

**Phainon:** Heh... They reached the same conclusion as the Theoros.

> **白厄：**呵…和神礼观众殊途同归啊。

**Gilgamesh:** And thus, a child of the gods was born with mixed blood and a heavy burden upon his shoulders. He was like a wedge cast down from heaven by the gods — the "Wedge of the Heavens" — and that is my true nature.

> **吉尔伽美什：**就这样，肩负重任的、混血的神子诞生了。他就像神在高天打入大地的楔子——也就是所谓「天之楔」——本王的本质。

**Lancer:** That may be so, but there are stories going around about how this child of the gods overthrew the gods with his dear friend.

> **Lancer：**话虽如此。但在传闻中，这家伙身为神之子，日后却和挚友一起推翻了诸神。

**Gilgamesh:** It is true that this body was crafted by the gods, but that is a separate matter from the being known as Gilgamesh.

> **吉尔伽美什：**此身为神创造不假，但与名为吉尔伽美什的生命是两回事。

**Gilgamesh:** Once the wind gusted into the opening and my soul struggled awake, I began my life as "me." Since I've decided to be a king, the land beneath my feet weighs deeply in my heart. As for the gods, why should I care for them?

> **吉尔伽美什：**自风吹入孔窍时起，在灵魂为苏醒而挣扎的瞬间，我就作为「我」而活了。既然认定自己为王，脚下的土地深得我心，神又与我何干？

**Phainon:** Hah, I understand now why I've been strangely drawn to you from the moment we met...

> **白厄：**呵，我总算明白了：为什么从见面起，我就能感受到某种奇妙的引力……

**Phainon:** We... walk the same path.

> **白厄：**原来，我们也殊途同归。

**Gilgamesh:** Hmph, my destiny is far more complicated than you can fathom: instructing my subjects, reveling in wealth, standing alongside my friend, eliminating all evils that hinder the development of civilization...

> **吉尔伽美什：**哼，本王的宿命远比你想象得更复杂：规诫臣民、拥享财富、与友人并肩而立，消灭妨碍文明发展的一切之恶……

**Phainon:** You're right. I take back what I just said. We walk different paths indeed.

> **白厄：**你说得对。我收回前言，殊途不同归哈。

**Gilgamesh:** Certainly so. I am the king, and trivial matters such as those were handled in the blink of an eye—

> *That great war has finally drawn to a close, and my journey is also reaching its conclusion. The most gripping part of a story is often not the climax. Instead, it is the more ordinary yet lingering aftertaste that follows. "What remains after a story comes to a close, after all material gains and losses are accounted for... something that seems to spring into being from nothing at all." What exactly is the answer to this question for me? Hehe, I will let you be the judge of that, Master.*
